Appendix A: Istio CRD Quick Reference
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    [TABLE: All Istio CRDs with purpose, API group, key fields, and example]

    ┌───────────────────────┬────────────┬──────────────────────────────────┬───────────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────┐
    │ CRD                   │ API Group  │ Purpose                          │ Key Fields                    │ One-line Example           │
    ├───────────────────────┼────────────┼──────────────────────────────────┼───────────────────────────────┼────────────────────────────┤
    │ VirtualService        │ networking │ Traffic routing rules            │ hosts, gateways, http.route   │ 90/10 canary jobpost-api   │
    │ DestinationRule       │ networking │ Traffic policies, subsets, LB    │ host, subsets, trafficPolicy  │ LEAST_CONN + CB, subsets   │
    │ Gateway               │ networking │ Mesh ingress/egress              │ selector, servers.port, tls   │ expose anihpj.example.com  │
    │ ServiceEntry          │ networking │ Register external services       │ hosts, location, resolution   │ register api.stripe.com    │
    │ Sidecar               │ networking │ Sidecar proxy scope/config       │ workloadSelector, egress      │ limit egress to anihpj/*   │
    │ EnvoyFilter           │ networking │ Low-level Envoy config override  │ configPatches, priority       │ add Lua response header    │
    │ WorkloadEntry         │ networking │ Register VM/external workload    │ address, labels, network      │ enroll VM 10.0.0.7         │
    │ WorkloadGroup         │ networking │ VM workload template             │ template, probe, metadata     │ VM auto-registration spec  │
    │ PeerAuthentication    │ security   │ mTLS mode per namespace/workload │ mtls.mode, portLevelMtls      │ STRICT for ns anihpj       │
    │ RequestAuthentication │ security   │ JWT validation policy            │ jwtRules: issuer, jwksUri     │ verify auth-service JWTs   │
    │ AuthorizationPolicy   │ security   │ Access control (allow/deny)      │ action, rules from/to/when    │ deny-all: spec {}          │
    │ Telemetry             │ telemetry  │ Metrics/tracing/logging config   │ tracing/metrics/accessLogging │ 1% sampling in anihpj      │
    │ ProxyConfig           │ networking │ Per-workload proxy configuration │ concurrency, image, selector  │ proxy concurrency: 2       │
    │ WasmPlugin            │ extensions │ Wasm modules in proxy filters    │ url, phase, pluginConfig      │ header-auth Wasm filter    │
    └───────────────────────┴────────────┴──────────────────────────────────┴───────────────────────────────┴────────────────────────────┘

         - How to read the table: PURPOSE answers "what problem does this
           CRD solve?", KEY FIELDS are the spec fields you actually type on
           the exam, and the EXAMPLE column names the exact anihpj manifest
           that uses it (each one is rendered in full in Appendix B).
         - Group shorthand: networking / security / telemetry / extensions
           are the API groups networking.istio.io, security.istio.io,
           telemetry.istio.io and extensions.istio.io.
         - DEFINITION: a CRD (Custom Resource Definition) teaches the
           Kubernetes API a new object type. Istio ships ~14 of them;
           istiod watches them and turns every change into live Envoy
           configuration pushed over xDS.

    [APIVERSION CHEAT — the first line of every manifest (Istio 1.22+)]
    ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
    │  networking.istio.io/v1        VirtualService, DestinationRule,    │
    │                                Gateway, ServiceEntry, Sidecar,     │
    │                                WorkloadEntry, WorkloadGroup,       │
    │                                ProxyConfig                         │
    │  security.istio.io/v1          PeerAuthentication,                 │
    │                                RequestAuthentication,              │
    │                                AuthorizationPolicy                 │
    │  telemetry.istio.io/v1         Telemetry                           │
    │  extensions.istio.io/v1alpha1  WasmPlugin   (still alpha)          │
    │  networking.istio.io/v1alpha3  EnvoyFilter  (still alpha)          │
    │                                                                    │
    │  Older v1alpha3/v1beta1 manifests still apply cleanly — v1 is      │
    │  the same schema promoted to stable, not a breaking change.        │
    └────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘


Appendix B: Complete anihpj Istio YAML Manifests
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    B.1  Namespace with istio-injection label
    B.2  Dockerfiles for all anihpj services (jobpost-api, user-service, etc.)
    B.3  Kubernetes Deployments + Services (with correct Istio labels/port names)
    B.4  Istio Gateway for anihpj.example.com
    B.5  VirtualService — path-based routing for all services
    B.6  VirtualService — canary deployment for jobpost-api (90/10 split)
    B.7  DestinationRule — subsets v1/v2, LEAST_CONN, circuit breaker
    B.8  PeerAuthentication — STRICT mTLS for anihpj namespace
    B.9  AuthorizationPolicy — deny-all baseline
    B.10 AuthorizationPolicy — allow rules per service pair
    B.11 ServiceEntry — external payment API
    B.12 RequestAuthentication — JWT validation for API endpoints
    B.13 Telemetry — custom sampling rate + metrics labels
    [COMPLETE COMMENTED YAML BLOCKS for each of the above]

    [B.1 — Namespace Setup]
    ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
    │  # Create namespace and enable Istio sidecar injection             │
    │  apiVersion: v1                                                    │
    │  kind: Namespace                                                   │
    │  metadata:                                                         │
    │    name: anihpj                                                    │
    │    labels:                                                         │
    │      istio-injection: enabled    ← KEY: enables auto injection     │
    │      environment: production                                       │
    │  ---                                                               │
    │  # Verify after applying:                                          │
    │  kubectl get namespace anihpj --show-labels                        │
    │  # Expected:                                                       │
    │  # NAME      STATUS   LABELS                                       │
    │  # anihpj    Active   istio-injection=enabled,environment=prod     │
    └────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

    [B.2 — Dockerfile for anihpj/jobpost-api]
    ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
    │  # anihpj/jobpost/Dockerfile                                       │
    │                                                                    │
    │  FROM python:3.11-slim                                             │
    │                                                                    │
    │  # Set working directory                                           │
    │  WORKDIR /app                                                      │
    │                                                                    │
    │  # Install dependencies first (Docker layer cache optimization)   │
    │  COPY requirements.txt .                                           │
    │  RUN pip install --no-cache-dir -r requirements.txt               │
    │                                                                    │
    │  # Copy application code                                          │
    │  COPY . .                                                          │
    │                                                                    │
    │  # Collect static files                                           │
    │  RUN python manage.py collectstatic --noinput                     │
    │                                                                    │
    │  # Health check endpoint (required for Kubernetes liveness probe) │
    │  HEALTHCHECK --interval=30s --timeout=10s --retries=3 \           │
    │    CMD curl -f http://localhost:8000/health/ || exit 1             │
    │                                                                    │
    │  # Run gunicorn (NOT Django dev server in production!)            │
    │  # Port 8000 — must match containerPort in Deployment            │
    │  EXPOSE 8000                                                       │
    │  CMD ["gunicorn", "--bind", "0.0.0.0:8000",                       │
    │       "--workers", "4", "--timeout", "120",                        │
    │       "anihpj.wsgi:application"]                                  │
    │                                                                    │
    │  # Build commands:                                                 │
    │  # docker build -t anihpj/jobpost-api:v1 .                        │
    │  # docker build -t anihpj/jobpost-api:v2 .  (for canary version)  │
    │  # docker push anihpj/jobpost-api:v1                              │
    └────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

    [anihpj DOCKER IMAGE FLOW — from code to Kubernetes Pod]
    ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
    │                                                                    │
    │  anihpj/jobpost/views.py        (your Django code)                 │
    │         │                                                          │
    │         ▼ docker build -t anihpj/jobpost-api:v1 .                 │
    │  Docker Image: anihpj/jobpost-api:v1                               │
    │  Contents: Python 3.11 + Django + gunicorn + your app code        │
    │         │                                                          │
    │         ▼ docker push → Container Registry (Docker Hub / ECR)     │
    │  Registry: docker.io/anihpj/jobpost-api:v1                        │
    │         │                                                          │
    │         ▼ kubectl apply -f deployment.yaml                         │
    │  Kubernetes pulls image → creates Pod                              │
    │         │                                                          │
    │         ▼ Istio Mutating Webhook fires (namespace: istio-injection=enabled)
    │  Pod gets MODIFIED:                                                │
    │  ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐  │
    │  │ Container 1: anihpj/jobpost-api:v1 (your code on port 8000)│  │
    │  │ Container 2: istio/proxyv2:1.22   (Envoy sidecar)          │  │
    │  │ InitContainer: istio-init         (sets iptables rules)     │  │
    │  └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘  │
    │         │                                                          │
    │         ▼                                                          │
    │  Pod running: 2/2 containers READY                                │
    │  kubectl get pods -n anihpj → jobpost-api-xxx   2/2   Running    │
    │                                                                    │
    └────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

    [B.3 — Complete Deployment + Service YAML for all anihpj services]
    ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
    │  apiVersion: apps/v1                                               │
    │  kind: Deployment                                                  │
    │  metadata:                                                         │
    │    name: jobpost-api                                               │
    │    namespace: anihpj                                               │
    │  spec:                                                             │
    │    replicas: 3                                                     │
    │    selector:                                                       │
    │      matchLabels:                                                  │
    │        app: jobpost-api                                            │
    │        version: v1                                                 │
    │    template:                                                       │
    │      metadata:                                                     │
    │        labels:                                                     │
    │          app: jobpost-api      ← REQUIRED for Kiali/Istio          │
    │          version: v1           ← REQUIRED for canary subsets       │
    │      spec:                                                         │
    │        containers:                                                 │
    │        - name: jobpost                                             │
    │          image: anihpj/jobpost-api:v1                              │
    │          ports:                                                    │
    │          - name: http          ← REQUIRED: Istio uses port name    │
    │            containerPort: 8000                                     │
    │          resources:                                                │
    │            requests:                                               │
    │              cpu: "100m"                                           │
    │              memory: "256Mi"                                       │
    │            limits:                                                 │
    │              cpu: "500m"                                           │
    │              memory: "512Mi"                                       │
    │          readinessProbe:                                           │
    │            httpGet:                                                │
    │              path: /health/                                        │
    │              port: 8000                                            │
    │  ---                                                               │
    │  apiVersion: v1                                                    │
    │  kind: Service                                                     │
    │  metadata:                                                         │
    │    name: jobpost-api           ← VirtualService references this   │
    │    namespace: anihpj                                               │
    │  spec:                                                             │
    │    selector:                                                       │
    │      app: jobpost-api          ← matches Deployment label         │
    │    ports:                                                          │
    │    - name: http                ← REQUIRED: must be "http"         │
    │      port: 80                                                      │
    │      targetPort: 8000                                              │
    └────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

    [B.4 — Istio Gateway: anihpj.example.com (HTTP 80 + HTTPS 443)]
    ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
    │  # The Gateway opens ports on the ingress-gateway pods.            │
    │  # It only ACCEPTS traffic — a VirtualService (B.5) routes it.     │
    │  apiVersion: networking.istio.io/v1                                │
    │  kind: Gateway                                                     │
    │  metadata:                                                         │
    │    name: anihpj-gateway                                            │
    │    namespace: anihpj             ← any namespace works             │
    │  spec:                                                             │
    │    selector:                                                       │
    │      istio: ingressgateway       ← targets the gateway Deployment  │
    │    servers:                                                        │
    │    - port:                                                         │
    │        number: 80                                                  │
    │        name: http                                                  │
    │        protocol: HTTP                                              │
    │      hosts:                                                        │
    │      - "anihpj.example.com"                                        │
    │      tls:                                                          │
    │        httpsRedirect: true       ← 301 every HTTP call to HTTPS    │
    │    - port:                                                         │
    │        number: 443                                                 │
    │        name: https                                                 │
    │        protocol: HTTPS                                             │
    │      tls:                                                          │
    │        mode: SIMPLE              ← gateway terminates TLS          │
    │        credentialName: anihpj-tls-cert   ← K8s TLS secret name     │
    │      hosts:                                                        │
    │      - "anihpj.example.com"                                        │
    │  # The secret must live in the ingress-gateway POD's namespace:    │
    │  # kubectl create secret tls anihpj-tls-cert                       │
    │  #   --cert=fullchain.pem --key=privkey.pem -n istio-system        │
    └────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

    [B.5 — VirtualService: path-based routing for ALL anihpj services]
    ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
    │  apiVersion: networking.istio.io/v1                                │
    │  kind: VirtualService                                              │
    │  metadata:                                                         │
    │    name: anihpj-routes                                             │
    │    namespace: anihpj                                               │
    │  spec:                                                             │
    │    hosts:                                                          │
    │    - "anihpj.example.com"        ← must match a Gateway host       │
    │    gateways:                                                       │
    │    - anihpj-gateway              ← binds this VS to B.4            │
    │    http:                         # FIRST MATCH WINS — order matters│
    │    - match:                                                        │
    │      - uri:                                                        │
    │          prefix: /jobs/                                            │
    │      route:                                                        │
    │      - destination:                                                │
    │          host: jobpost-api       ← K8s Service (:80 → pod :8000)   │
    │    - match:                                                        │
    │      - uri:                                                        │
    │          prefix: /users/                                           │
    │      route:                                                        │
    │      - destination:                                                │
    │          host: user-service      ← :8080                           │
    │    - match:                                                        │
    │      - uri:                                                        │
    │          prefix: /skills/                                          │
    │      route:                                                        │
    │      - destination:                                                │
    │          host: skills-service    ← :8090                           │
    │    - match:                                                        │
    │      - uri:                                                        │
    │          prefix: /locations/                                       │
    │      route:                                                        │
    │      - destination:                                                │
    │          host: location-service  ← :8070                           │
    │    - match:                                                        │
    │      - uri:                                                        │
    │          prefix: /auth/                                            │
    │      route:                                                        │
    │      - destination:                                                │
    │          host: auth-service                                        │
    │    - match:                                                        │
    │      - uri:                                                        │
    │          prefix: /search/                                          │
    │      route:                                                        │
    │      - destination:                                                │
    │          host: search-service                                      │
    │    - match:                                                        │
    │      - uri:                                                        │
    │          prefix: /notifications/                                   │
    │      route:                                                        │
    │      - destination:                                                │
    │          host: notification-service                                │
    │    - route:                      ← catch-all: NO match block, LAST │
    │      - destination:                                                │
    │          host: jobpost-api                                         │
    └────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

    [B.6 — VirtualService: canary 90/10 for jobpost-api]
    ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
    │  apiVersion: networking.istio.io/v1                                │
    │  kind: VirtualService                                              │
    │  metadata:                                                         │
    │    name: jobpost-api-canary                                        │
    │    namespace: anihpj                                               │
    │  spec:                                                             │
    │    hosts:                                                          │
    │    - jobpost-api                 ← mesh-internal host (K8s Service)│
    │    http:                                                           │
    │    - route:                                                        │
    │      - destination:                                                │
    │          host: jobpost-api                                         │
    │          subset: v1              ← must exist in the DR (B.7)      │
    │        weight: 90                ← 90% stay on stable v1           │
    │      - destination:                                                │
    │          host: jobpost-api                                         │
    │          subset: v2                                                │
    │        weight: 10                ← 10% try v2; weights sum to 100  │
    │  # Ramp: 10 → 25 → 50 → 100 while watching Kiali/Grafana.          │
    │  # Instant rollback: set the v2 weight back to 0 and re-apply.     │
    └────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

    [B.7 — DestinationRule: subsets + LEAST_CONN + circuit breaker]
    ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
    │  apiVersion: networking.istio.io/v1                                │
    │  kind: DestinationRule                                             │
    │  metadata:                                                         │
    │    name: jobpost-api-dr                                            │
    │    namespace: anihpj                                               │
    │  spec:                                                             │
    │    host: jobpost-api                                               │
    │    trafficPolicy:                                                  │
    │      loadBalancer:                                                 │
    │        simple: LEAST_CONN        ← route to the least-busy pod     │
    │      connectionPool:                                               │
    │        tcp:                                                        │
    │          maxConnections: 100     ← hard cap on TCP connections     │
    │        http:                                                       │
    │          http1MaxPendingRequests: 50   ← queue depth before 503    │
    │      outlierDetection:           ← the CIRCUIT BREAKER             │
    │        consecutiveGatewayErrors: 5     ← 5 straight 502/503/504    │
    │        interval: 30s             ← how often hosts are scanned     │
    │        baseEjectionTime: 30s     ← ejected 30s, doubles each trip  │
    │        maxEjectionPercent: 50    ← never eject more than half      │
    │      tls:                                                          │
    │        mode: ISTIO_MUTUAL        ← use Istio-issued mTLS certs     │
    │    subsets:                                                        │
    │    - name: v1                                                      │
    │      labels:                                                       │
    │        version: v1               ← selects pods with version=v1    │
    │    - name: v2                                                      │
    │      labels:                                                       │
    │        version: v2               ← the canary pods                 │
    └────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

    [B.8 — PeerAuthentication: STRICT mTLS namespace-wide]
    ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
    │  apiVersion: security.istio.io/v1                                  │
    │  kind: PeerAuthentication                                          │
    │  metadata:                                                         │
    │    name: default                 ← naming convention for ns-wide   │
    │    namespace: anihpj             ← applies to every workload here  │
    │  spec:                                                             │
    │    mtls:                                                           │
    │      mode: STRICT                ← mTLS only; plaintext gets a     │
    │                                    TCP connection RESET (not 403)  │
    │  # No selector: → namespace-wide. Add spec.selector.matchLabels    │
    │  # to scope one workload. Verify: istioctl x describe pod <pod>    │
    └────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

    [B.9 — AuthorizationPolicy: deny-all baseline (zero trust)]
    ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
    │  apiVersion: security.istio.io/v1                                  │
    │  kind: AuthorizationPolicy                                         │
    │  metadata:                                                         │
    │    name: deny-all                                                  │
    │    namespace: anihpj                                               │
    │  spec: {}                        ← empty spec = DENY EVERYTHING    │
    │  # Why it works: an ALLOW policy with no rules matches nothing,    │
    │  # and once ANY policy selects a workload, all traffic that no     │
    │  # ALLOW rule matches is denied.                                   │
    │  # Callers now see: HTTP 403 "RBAC: access denied".                │
    │  # Apply this FIRST, then open pairs one by one (B.10).            │
    └────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

    [B.10 — AuthorizationPolicy: allow rules per service pair]
    ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
    │  # Pair 1: ingress gateway → jobpost-api (GET/POST only)           │
    │  apiVersion: security.istio.io/v1                                  │
    │  kind: AuthorizationPolicy                                         │
    │  metadata:                                                         │
    │    name: allow-ingress-to-jobpost                                  │
    │    namespace: anihpj                                               │
    │  spec:                                                             │
    │    selector:                                                       │
    │      matchLabels:                                                  │
    │        app: jobpost-api          ← policy attaches to the SERVER   │
    │    action: ALLOW                                                   │
    │    rules:                                                          │
    │    - from:                                                         │
    │      - source:                                                     │
    │          principals:             ← SPIFFE identity of the CALLER   │
    │          - "cluster.local/ns/istio-system/sa/istio-ingressgateway" │
    │      to:                                                           │
    │      - operation:                                                  │
    │          methods: ["GET", "POST"]                                  │
    │  ---                                                               │
    │  # Pair 2: jobpost-api → user-service (GET on /api/users/* only)   │
    │  apiVersion: security.istio.io/v1                                  │
    │  kind: AuthorizationPolicy                                         │
    │  metadata:                                                         │
    │    name: allow-jobpost-to-users                                    │
    │    namespace: anihpj                                               │
    │  spec:                                                             │
    │    selector:                                                       │
    │      matchLabels:                                                  │
    │        app: user-service                                           │
    │    action: ALLOW                                                   │
    │    rules:                                                          │
    │    - from:                                                         │
    │      - source:                                                     │
    │          principals:                                               │
    │          - "cluster.local/ns/anihpj/sa/jobpost-api"                │
    │      to:                                                           │
    │      - operation:                                                  │
    │          methods: ["GET"]                                          │
    │          paths: ["/api/users/*"]                                   │
    │  # Every OTHER pair stays 403-denied by B.9's deny-all.            │
    └────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

    [B.11 — ServiceEntry: external payment API (api.stripe.com)]
    ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
    │  apiVersion: networking.istio.io/v1                                │
    │  kind: ServiceEntry                                                │
    │  metadata:                                                         │
    │    name: stripe-payments                                           │
    │    namespace: anihpj                                               │
    │  spec:                                                             │
    │    hosts:                                                          │
    │    - api.stripe.com              ← the DNS name the app calls      │
    │    ports:                                                          │
    │    - number: 443                                                   │
    │      name: https                                                   │
    │      protocol: HTTPS                                               │
    │    location: MESH_EXTERNAL       ← lives outside; no sidecar there │
    │    resolution: DNS               ← resolve the name at call time   │
    │  # Required when outboundTrafficPolicy.mode = REGISTRY_ONLY —      │
    │  # without it, payment calls die with 502. Bonus: Stripe traffic   │
    │  # now shows up in Kiali/Grafana like any mesh service.            │
    └────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

    [B.12 — RequestAuthentication: JWT validation for API endpoints]
    ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
    │  apiVersion: security.istio.io/v1                                  │
    │  kind: RequestAuthentication                                       │
    │  metadata:                                                         │
    │    name: jwt-auth                                                  │
    │    namespace: anihpj                                               │
    │  spec:                                                             │
    │    selector:                                                       │
    │      matchLabels:                                                  │
    │        app: jobpost-api          ← check JWTs arriving here        │
    │    jwtRules:                                                       │
    │    - issuer: "auth.anihpj.example.com"   ← must equal token "iss"  │
    │      jwksUri: "http://auth-service:8080/.well-known/jwks.json"     │
    │      forwardOriginalToken: true  ← Django still sees the JWT       │
    │  ---                                                               │
    │  # GOTCHA: RequestAuthentication alone only rejects INVALID        │
    │  # tokens (401). Requests with NO token still pass! Enforce        │
    │  # token presence with an AuthorizationPolicy:                     │
    │  apiVersion: security.istio.io/v1                                  │
    │  kind: AuthorizationPolicy                                         │
    │  metadata:                                                         │
    │    name: require-jwt                                               │
    │    namespace: anihpj                                               │
    │  spec:                                                             │
    │    selector:                                                       │
    │      matchLabels:                                                  │
    │        app: jobpost-api                                            │
    │    action: ALLOW                                                   │
    │    rules:                                                          │
    │    - from:                                                         │
    │      - source:                                                     │
    │          requestPrincipals: ["*"]    ← any VALIDATED issuer/sub    │
    └────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

    [B.13 — Telemetry: 1% sampling + custom metric label]
    ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
    │  apiVersion: telemetry.istio.io/v1                                 │
    │  kind: Telemetry                                                   │
    │  metadata:                                                         │
    │    name: anihpj-telemetry                                          │
    │    namespace: anihpj             ← ns scope (istio-system = mesh)  │
    │  spec:                                                             │
    │    tracing:                                                        │
    │    - randomSamplingPercentage: 1.0   ← trace 1 in 100 requests     │
    │    metrics:                                                        │
    │    - providers:                                                    │
    │      - name: prometheus                                            │
    │      overrides:                                                    │
    │      - match:                                                      │
    │          metric: REQUEST_COUNT   ← istio_requests_total            │
    │        tagOverrides:                                               │
    │          job_category:           ← add a NEW label to the metric   │
    │            value: request.headers["x-job-category"]                │
    │  # 1% sampling keeps overhead tiny at anihpj's 847 req/s while     │
    │  # still catching slow paths like skills-service's 1240ms p99.     │
    └────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

    [APPLY ORDER — how the B.1-B.13 manifests fit together]
    ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
    │  B.1 namespace → B.2 images → B.3 deploy+svc     (app up, 2/2)     │
    │       │                                                            │
    │       ▼  traffic layer                                             │
    │  B.4 Gateway → B.5 routes → B.7 DR subsets → B.6 canary 90/10      │
    │                  (apply the DR BEFORE the canary VS — a VS         │
    │                   that names a missing subset returns 503)         │
    │       │                                                            │
    │       ▼  security layer                                            │
    │  B.8 STRICT mTLS → B.9 deny-all → B.10 allow pairs → B.12 JWT      │
    │       │                                                            │
    │       ▼  egress + observability                                    │
    │  B.11 ServiceEntry (Stripe) → B.13 Telemetry (1% sampling)         │
    └────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘


Appendix C: istioctl Command Reference
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    [TABLE organized by task category]

    INSTALLATION:
    ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
    │  istioctl install --set profile=demo -y                │
    │  istioctl install -f custom-iop.yaml                   │
    │  istioctl upgrade                                      │
    │  istioctl uninstall --purge                            │
    │  istioctl verify-install                               │
    └────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

    DEBUGGING:
    ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
    │  istioctl proxy-status                                  │
    │  istioctl proxy-config cluster <pod> -n <ns>           │
    │  istioctl proxy-config route <pod> -n <ns>             │
    │  istioctl proxy-config listener <pod> -n <ns>          │
    │  istioctl proxy-config endpoint <pod> -n <ns>          │
    │  istioctl analyze -n <ns>                              │
    │  istioctl analyze --all-namespaces                     │
    │  istioctl x describe pod <pod> -n <ns>                 │
    │  istioctl bug-report                                   │
    └────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

         - ⚠️ Old tutorials still mention
           "istioctl authn tls-check" — that command was REMOVED from
           istioctl years ago and NO LONGER EXISTS on a current cluster.
           Learn the modern replacements below, or you will burn exam
           minutes on "unknown command" errors.

    [REMOVED/RENAMED COMMANDS — do not use on the exam]
    ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
    │  GONE:  istioctl authn tls-check <pod> -n <ns>       (removed)     │
    │    use → istioctl x describe pod <pod> -n <ns>  effective mTLS     │
    │    use → istioctl proxy-config secret <pod> -n <ns>  live certs    │
    │    or  → Kiali graph lock icons / openssl s_client to pod port     │
    │                                                                    │
    │  GONE:  istioctl register / deregister               (removed)     │
    │    use → WorkloadEntry + WorkloadGroup CRDs for VM workloads       │
    │                                                                    │
    │  MOVED: istioctl experimental <cmd>  =  istioctl x <cmd>           │
    │    "x" is the alias; some x commands graduate to top level         │
    │                                                                    │
    │  RISKY: istioctl upgrade (in-place) — exists, but prefer the       │
    │    canary upgrade: install a new revision, relabel namespaces      │
    │    with istio.io/rev, restart workloads, uninstall old rev         │
    └────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

    DASHBOARDS:
    ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
    │  istioctl dashboard kiali                              │
    │  istioctl dashboard grafana                            │
    │  istioctl dashboard jaeger                             │
    │  istioctl dashboard prometheus                         │
    │  istioctl dashboard zipkin                             │
    │  istioctl dashboard controlz                           │
    │  istioctl dashboard envoy <pod> -n <ns>                │
    └────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘


Appendix D: Common Istio Errors and Solutions
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    [TABLE: Error → Cause → Diagnostic Command → Fix]

    | Error / Symptom                   | Likely Cause                        | Fix                                       |
    | ───────────────────────────────── | ─────────────────────────────────── | ───────────────────────────────────────── |
    | Pod shows 1/1 not 2/2 containers  | Sidecar not injected                | Label namespace: istio-injection=enabled  |
    | 503 RBAC: access denied           | AuthorizationPolicy blocking        | Add explicit ALLOW policy                 |
    | 503 upstream connect error        | DestinationRule mTLS mode wrong     | Match PeerAuthentication mode             |
    | VirtualService not routing        | Subset not in DestinationRule       | Add matching subset to DR                 |
    | Traffic still going to all pods   | Missing version: label on pods      | Add version label to Deployment           |
    | istioctl analyze: "host not found"| Service name mismatch in VS         | Match VS host to exact K8s svc name       |
    | High latency after Istio install  | Tracing 100% sampling overhead      | Set sampling to 1% in Telemetry           |
    | Circuit breaker tripping often    | outlierDetection too aggressive     | Increase consecutiveGatewayErrors         |
    | External API calls failing        | Mesh in REGISTRY_ONLY mode          | Add ServiceEntry for external host        |
    | PROXY_STATUS shows STALE          | Network issue to istiod             | Check istiod pod health                   |
    | Apply fails: webhook x509 error   | istiod webhook cert/CA not ready    | Check istiod up; svc 443→15017 reachable  |
    | EDS: endpoints list is empty      | Svc selector ≠ pod labels; not Ready| Fix Service selector / readinessProbe     |
    | 401 from RequestAuthentication    | issuer/jwksUri mismatch or bad token| Match issuer + jwksUri to the token iss   |
    | Locality failover not working     | No outlierDetection in DR (required)| Add outlierDetection to enable failover   |
    | Ambient pod shows 1/1 "no sidecar"| By design — ambient mode has none   | Normal: ztunnel proxies node traffic      |

    [FIRST-RESPONSE DIAGNOSTIC LADDER — run these in this order]
    ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
    │  1. istioctl analyze -n anihpj            config errors?           │
    │  2. istioctl proxy-status                 SYNCED or STALE?         │
    │  3. kubectl get pods -n anihpj            2/2 READY?               │
    │  4. istioctl x describe pod <pod>         which policies apply?    │
    │  5. istioctl proxy-config endpoint <pod>  endpoints exist?         │
    │  6. kubectl logs <pod> -c istio-proxy     Envoy access-log codes   │
    └────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘


Appendix E: Istio Terminology Glossary
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    [GLOSSARY TABLE: 40+ terms alphabetically sorted]

    | Term                   | Definition                                                 |
    | ────────────────────── | ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── |
    | ADS                    | Aggregated Discovery Service — one ordered xDS stream      |
    | Ambient Mesh           | Sidecar-free Istio mode using ztunnel + waypoint proxies   |
    | AuthorizationPolicy    | Istio CRD for access control (who can call what)           |
    | Canary Upgrade         | Run old + new istiod side by side; move ns via revision    |
    | Circuit Breaker        | Pattern: stop calls to a failing service automatically     |
    | Citadel                | istiod's built-in CA — signs and rotates workload certs    |
    | Control Plane          | istiod — distributes config, certs, policy to proxies      |
    | CRD                    | Custom Resource Definition — extending Kubernetes API      |
    | Data Plane             | All Envoy sidecar proxies handling actual traffic          |
    | DestinationRule        | Policies for traffic going TO a service (LB, mTLS, CB)     |
    | Dry-run                | Annotation istio.io/dry-run — test policy w/o enforcing    |
    | East-West Gateway      | Gateway for cross-cluster traffic in multi-cluster mesh    |
    | Egress Gateway         | Dedicated exit proxy — force outbound through one point    |
    | Envoy                  | High-performance L7 proxy; Istio's sidecar proxy           |
    | exportTo               | Field scoping which namespaces can see a VS/DR/SE          |
    | extensionProvider      | MeshConfig entry naming a tracing/logging/authz backend    |
    | Fault Injection        | Deliberately inject delays or errors to test resilience    |
    | Gateway (Istio)        | Entry/exit point to the mesh for external traffic          |
    | HBONE                  | HTTP-Based Overlay Network Encapsulation — ambient tunnel  |
    | istiod                 | Istio control plane: Pilot + Citadel + Galley in one pod   |
    | istioctl               | Istio command-line tool (like kubectl for Istio)           |
    | IstioOperator          | Install-time API: profiles + component overrides (IOP)     |
    | Jaeger                 | Distributed tracing backend; visualizes trace spans        |
    | Kiali                  | Istio mesh visualization and management console            |
    | Locality Load Balancing| Prefer same-zone pods; failover needs outlierDetection     |
    | mTLS                   | Mutual TLS — both parties verify certificates              |
    | Mutating Webhook       | K8s admission hook istiod uses to inject the sidecar       |
    | Outlier Detection      | DestinationRule setting that implements circuit breaking   |
    | PeerAuthentication     | Istio CRD configuring mTLS mode per namespace/workload     |
    | Pilot                  | istiod component: reads CRDs, configures Envoy via xDS     |
    | RED Metrics            | Rate, Errors, Duration — the three key service metrics     |
    | RequestAuthentication  | Istio CRD for JWT token validation at mesh level           |
    | Revision               | Named istiod install (e.g. 1-22) targeted by istio.io/rev  |
    | Secure Naming          | Maps service identity → service name; stops impersonation  |
    | Service Mesh           | Infrastructure layer for service-to-service communication  |
    | ServiceEntry           | Istio CRD to register external services in the mesh        |
    | Sidecar (container)    | Envoy proxy container injected alongside your app          |
    | Sidecar (resource)     | Istio CRD to configure sidecar proxy scope                 |
    | SPIFFE                 | Secure Production Identity Framework — cert standard used  |
    | SVID                   | SPIFFE Verifiable Identity Document — workload certificate |
    | Telemetry              | Istio CRD for configuring metrics, tracing, logging        |
    | Trust Domain           | Identity root of a mesh (default cluster.local)            |
    | VirtualService         | Istio CRD defining routing rules for a service             |
    | WasmPlugin             | CRD (extensions.istio.io) — run Wasm modules in the proxy  |
    | Waypoint Proxy         | Ambient mesh L7 proxy per namespace (no sidecar needed)    |
    | xDS API                | Protocol used by istiod to configure Envoy proxies         |
    | ztunnel                | Ambient mesh L4 proxy; handles mTLS without sidecars       |
